Prompt 1
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> List[int]:
    task_1_input.sort()
    n = len(task_1_input)
    f = [1] * n
    k = 0
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(i):
            if task_1_input[i] % task_1_input[j] == 0:
                f[i] = max(f[i], f[j] + 1)
        if f[k] < f[i]:
            k = i
    m = f[k]
    i = k
    ans = []
    while m:
        if task_1_input[k] % task_1_input[i] == 0 and f[i] == m:
            ans.append(task_1_input[i])
            (k, m) = (i, m - 1)
        i -= 1
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 9
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    ans = s = 0
    pos = {}
    for (i, x) in enumerate(task_1_input):
        s += 1 if x > 8 else -1
        if s > 0:
            ans = i + 1
        elif s - 1 in pos:
            ans = max(ans, i - pos[s - 1])
        if s not in pos:
            pos[s] = i
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 17
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Define task_1_passengers_static = [47, -7, -58, -15, 42, -97, -8, 22, -90, -97, -55, 0, -32, 37, -78, -7, -78, -65, -38, 55, 70, 64, 9, 71, 98, 40, -93, -92, -61, -35].
Define task_1_capacity_static = 55.
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs passengers = task_1_passengers_static, capacity = task_1_capacity_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], passengers: List[int], capacity: int) -> int:
    task_1_input.sort()
    passengers.sort()
    j = 0
    for t in task_1_input:
        c = capacity
        while c and j < len(passengers) and (passengers[j] <= t):
            (c, j) = (c - 1, j + 1)
    j -= 1
    ans = task_1_input[-1] if c else passengers[j]
    while ~j and passengers[j] == ans:
        (ans, j) = (ans - 1, j - 1)
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Define task_1_passengers_static = [18, -99, 89, 98, -28, -8, 61, -76, -48, 47, -69, -79, 15, -50, -93, -82, -1, 33, 41, -56, -29, -54, 72, -25, -38, -80, 83, 23, 51, 47].
Define task_1_capacity_static = -17.
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs passengers = task_1_passengers_static, capacity = task_1_capacity_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], passengers: List[int], capacity: int) -> int:
    task_1_input.sort()
    passengers.sort()
    j = 0
    for t in task_1_input:
        c = capacity
        while c and j < len(passengers) and (passengers[j] <= t):
            (c, j) = (c - 1, j + 1)
    j -= 1
    ans = task_1_input[-1] if c else passengers[j]
    while ~j and passengers[j] == ans:
        (ans, j) = (ans - 1, j - 1)
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    (left, right) = (0, len(task_1_input) - 1)
    while left < right:
        mid = left + right >> 1
        if task_1_input[mid] > task_1_input[right]:
            left = mid + 1
        elif task_1_input[mid] < task_1_input[right]:
            right = mid
        else:
            right -= 1
    python_result_1 = task_1_input[left]
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 33
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    (i, j) = (0, len(task_1_input) - 1)
    (a, b) = (task_1_input[i], task_1_input[j])
    ans = 0
    while i < j:
        if a < b:
            i += 1
            a += task_1_input[i]
            ans += 1
        elif b < a:
            j -= 1
            b += task_1_input[j]
            ans += 1
        else:
            (i, j) = (i + 1, j - 1)
            (a, b) = (task_1_input[i], task_1_input[j])
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 41
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Define task_1_queries_static = [-31, 30, -92, 98, -50, -59, -17, 4, -50, -97, 18, 51, 90, 16, 29, -29, 52, 67, 3, -34, 73, 61, 24, -33, 56, 56, -94, 46, -67, -75].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs queries = task_1_queries_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], queries: List[int]) -> int:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    f = [[0] * n for _ in range(n)]
    m = len(queries)
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(n - 1, i - 1, -1):
            if i:
                f[i][j] = max(f[i][j], f[i - 1][j] + (task_1_input[i - 1] >= queries[f[i - 1][j]]))
            if j + 1 < n:
                f[i][j] = max(f[i][j], f[i][j + 1] + (task_1_input[j + 1] >= queries[f[i][j + 1]]))
            if f[i][j] == m:
                python_result_1 = m
                return python_result_1
    python_result_1 = max((f[i][i] + (task_1_input[i] >= queries[f[i][i]]) for i in range(n)))
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

```python
    m = len(queries)
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(n - 1, i - 1, -1):
            if i:
                f[i][j] = max(f[i][j], f[i - 1][j] + (task_1_input[i - 1] >= queries[f[i - 1][j]]))
            if j + 1 < n:
                f[i][j] = max(f[i][j], f[i][j + 1] + (task_1_input[j + 1] >= queries[f[i][j + 1]]))
            if f[i][j] == m:
                python_result_1 = m
                return python_result_1
    python_result_1 = max((f[i][i] + (task_1_input[i] >= queries[f[i][i]]) for i in range(n)))
    return python_result_1
```

Prompt 49
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Define task_1_limit_static = 59.
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs limit = task_1_limit_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], limit: int) -> List[int]:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    arr = sorted(zip(task_1_input, range(n)))
    ans = [0] * n
    i = 0
    while i < n:
        j = i + 1
        while j < n and arr[j][0] - arr[j - 1][0] <= limit:
            j += 1
        idx = sorted((k for (_, k) in arr[i:j]))
        for (k, (x, _)) in zip(idx, arr[i:j]):
            ans[k] = x
        i = j
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 57
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    stk = []
    ans = 0
    for x in task_1_input:
        while stk and stk[-1][0] < x:
            stk.pop()
        if not stk or stk[-1][0] > x:
            stk.append([x, 1])
        else:
            stk[-1][1] += 1
        ans += stk[-1][1]
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

```python
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    stk = []
    ans = 0
    for x in task_1_input:
        while stk and stk[-1][0] < x:
            stk.pop()
        if not stk or stk[-1][0] > x:
            stk.append([x, 1])
        else:
            stk[-1][1] += 1
        ans += stk[-1][1]
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
```
```python
    ans = [0, 0]
    n = len(task_1_input)
    for i in range(2):
        for j in range(i, n, 2):
            d = 0
            if j:
                d = max(d, task_1_input[j] - task_1_input[j - 1] + 1)
            if j < n - 1:
                d = max(d, task_1_input[j] - task_1_input[j + 1] + 1)
            ans[i] += d
    python_result_1 = min(ans)
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.

Prompt 73
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> List[int]:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    left = [-1] * n
    right = [n] * n
    stk = []
    for (i, x) in enumerate(task_1_input):
        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] >= x:
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            left[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(i)
    stk = []
    for i in range(n - 1, -1, -1):
        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] >= task_1_input[i]:
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            right[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(i)
    ans = [0] * n
    for i in range(n):
        m = right[i] - left[i] - 1
        ans[m - 1] = max(ans[m - 1], task_1_input[i])
    for i in range(n - 2, -1, -1):
        ans[i] = max(ans[i], ans[i + 1])
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

```python
from typing import List
```
```python
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    ans = j = 0
    for i in range(1, len(task_1_input) - 1):
        if task_1_input[i] == task_1_input[i + 1]:
            continue
        if task_1_input[i] > task_1_input[j] and task_1_input[i] > task_1_input[i + 1]:
            ans += 1
        if task_1_input[i] < task_1_input[j] and task_1_input[i] < task_1_input[i + 1]:
            ans += 1
        j = i
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```

```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
```
```python
    n = len(task_1_input)
    ans = 0
    for k in range(1, n + 1):
        t = 0
        j = 1
        while k * j * j <= n:
            t += task_1_input[k * j * j - 1]
            j += 1
        ans = max(ans, t)
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.

Prompt 97
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    left = [1] * n
    right = [1] * n
    for i in range(1, n):
        if task_1_input[i] > task_1_input[i - 1]:
            left[i] = left[i - 1] + 1
    for i in range(n - 2, -1, -1):
        if task_1_input[i] > task_1_input[i + 1]:
            right[i] = right[i + 1] + 1
    python_result_1 = sum((max(a, b) for (a, b) in zip(left, right)))
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 105
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> List[int]:
    stk = []
    for x in task_1_input:
        if x > 0:
            stk.append(x)
        else:
            while stk and stk[-1] > 0 and (stk[-1] < -x):
                stk.pop()
            if stk and stk[-1] == -x:
                stk.pop()
            elif not stk or stk[-1] < 0:
                stk.append(x)
    python_result_1 = stk
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> List[int]:
    stk = []
    for x in task_1_input:
        if x > 0:
            stk.append(x)
        else:
            while stk and stk[-1] > 0 and (stk[-1] < -x):
                stk.pop()
            if stk and stk[-1] == -x:
                stk.pop()
            elif not stk or stk[-1] < 0:
                stk.append(x)
    python_result_1 = stk
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 113
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    stk = []
    for (i, v) in enumerate(task_1_input):
        if not stk or task_1_input[stk[-1]] > v:
            stk.append(i)
    ans = 0
    for i in range(len(task_1_input) - 1, -1, -1):
        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] <= task_1_input[i]:
            ans = max(ans, i - stk.pop())
        if not stk:
            break
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 121
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> List[int]:
    f = True
    while f:
        f = False
        t = task_1_input[:]
        for i in range(1, len(t) - 1):
            if t[i] > t[i - 1] and t[i] > t[i + 1]:
                task_1_input[i] -= 1
                f = True
            if t[i] < t[i - 1] and t[i] < t[i + 1]:
                task_1_input[i] += 1
                f = True
    python_result_1 = task_1_input
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 129
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    left = [0] * (n + 1)
    right = [0] * (n + 1)
    for (i, x) in enumerate(task_1_input, 1):
        if x:
            left[i] = left[i - 1] + 1
    for i in range(n - 1, -1, -1):
        if task_1_input[i]:
            right[i] = right[i + 1] + 1
    python_result_1 = max((left[i] + right[i + 1] for i in range(n)))
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

```python
        if x:
            left[i] = left[i - 1] + 1
    for i in range(n - 1, -1, -1):
        if task_1_input[i]:
            right[i] = right[i + 1] + 1
    python_result_1 = max((left[i] + right[i + 1] for i in range(n)))
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 145
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    f = [1] * n
    g = [1] * n
    for i in range(1, n):
        if task_1_input[i] > task_1_input[i - 1]:
            f[i] = f[i - 1] + 1
    ans = 0
    for i in range(n - 2, -1, -1):
        if task_1_input[i] > task_1_input[i + 1]:
            g[i] = g[i + 1] + 1
            if f[i] > 1:
                ans = max(ans, f[i] + g[i] - 1)
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 153
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> List[int]:
    (i, j) = (0, len(task_1_input) - 1)
    while i < j:
        if task_1_input[i] % 2 == 0:
            i += 1
        elif task_1_input[j] % 2 == 1:
            j -= 1
        else:
            (task_1_input[i], task_1_input[j]) = (task_1_input[j], task_1_input[i])
            (i, j) = (i + 1, j - 1)
    python_result_1 = task_1_input
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> List[int]:
    (i, j) = (0, len(task_1_input) - 1)
    while i < j:
        if task_1_input[i] % 2 == 0:
            i += 1
        elif task_1_input[j] % 2 == 1:
            j -= 1
        else:
            (task_1_input[i], task_1_input[j]) = (task_1_input[j], task_1_input[i])
            (i, j) = (i + 1, j - 1)
    python_result_1 = task_1_input
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 161
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
def task_1(task_1_input: int) -> int:
    s = list(str(task_1_input))
    i = 1
    while i < len(s) and s[i - 1] <= s[i]:
        i += 1
    if i < len(s):
        while i and s[i - 1] > s[i]:
            s[i - 1] = str(int(s[i - 1]) - 1)
            i -= 1
        i += 1
        while i < len(s):
            s[i] = '9'
            i += 1
    python_result_1 = int(''.join(s))
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 169
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    ans = 1
    f = [1] * n
    g = [1] * n
    for i in range(1, n):
        for j in range(i):
            if task_1_input[j] < task_1_input[i]:
                f[i] = max(f[i], g[j] + 1)
            elif task_1_input[j] > task_1_input[i]:
                g[i] = max(g[i], f[j] + 1)
        ans = max(ans, f[i], g[i])
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs k = task_1_k_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], k: int) -> int:
    (ans, y) = (1, task_1_input[0])
    for x in task_1_input[1:]:
        if x == 0:
            python_result_1 = 1
            return python_result_1
        if x * y <= k:
            y *= x
        else:
            y = x
            ans += 1
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 185
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    def f(x: int) -> int:
        i = 2
        (cnt, s) = (2, x + 1)
        while i <= x // i:
            if x % i == 0:
                cnt += 1
                s += i
                if i * i != x:
                    cnt += 1
                    s += x // i
            i += 1
        return s if cnt == 4 else 0
    python_result_1 = sum((f(x) for x in task_1_input))
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 193
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> List[int]:
    def reverse(arr, j):
        i = 0
        while i < j:
            (arr[i], arr[j]) = (arr[j], arr[i])
            (i, j) = (i + 1, j - 1)
    n = len(task_1_input)
    ans = []
    for i in range(n - 1, 0, -1):
        j = i
        while j > 0 and task_1_input[j] != i + 1:
            j -= 1
        if j < i:
            if j > 0:
                ans.append(j + 1)
                reverse(task_1_input, j)
            ans.append(i + 1)
            reverse(task_1_input, i)
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 201
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    if task_1_input[0] <= task_1_input[-1]:
        python_result_1 = task_1_input[0]
        return python_result_1
    (left, right) = (0, len(task_1_input) - 1)
    while left < right:
        mid = left + right >> 1
        if task_1_input[0] <= task_1_input[mid]:
            left = mid + 1
        else:
            right = mid
    python_result_1 = task_1_input[left]
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

```python
        python_result_1 = task_1_input[0]
        return python_result_1
    (left, right) = (0, len(task_1_input) - 1)
    while left < right:
        mid = left + right >> 1
        if task_1_input[0] <= task_1_input[mid]:
            left = mid + 1
        else:
            right = mid
    python_result_1 = task_1_input[left]
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
```
```python
    (l, r) = (0, len(task_1_input) - 1)
    ans = 0
    while l < r:
        t = min(task_1_input[l], task_1_input[r]) * (r - l)
        ans = max(ans, t)
        if task_1_input[l] < task_1_input[r]:
            l += 1
        else:
            r -= 1
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.

Prompt 217
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    left = [0] * n
    right = [0] * n
    s = 0
    for (i, x) in enumerate(task_1_input):
        s = max(s, 0) + x
        left[i] = s
    s = 0
    for i in range(n - 1, -1, -1):
        s = max(s, 0) + task_1_input[i]
        right[i] = s
    ans = max(left)
    for i in range(1, n - 1):
        ans = max(ans, left[i - 1] + right[i + 1])
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

======================================================================
Prompt 225
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    stk = []
    left = [-1] * n
    right = [n] * n
    for (i, h) in enumerate(task_1_input):
        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] >= h:
            right[stk[-1]] = i
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            left[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(i)
    python_result_1 = max((h * (right[i] - left[i] - 1) for (i, h) in enumerate(task_1_input)))
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    stk = []
    left = [-1] * n
    right = [n] * n
    for (i, h) in enumerate(task_1_input):
        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] >= h:
            right[stk[-1]] = i
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            left[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(i)
    python_result_1 = max((h * (right[i] - left[i] - 1) for (i, h) in enumerate(task_1_input)))
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:

    stk = []

    right = [n] * n

            right[stk[-1]] = i
            stk.pop()

    return python_result_1
```

```python
    for j in range(1, n):
        if task_1_input[i] < task_1_input[j]:
            i = j
            cnt = 1
        else:
            cnt += 1
        if cnt == k:
            break
    python_result_1 = i
    return python_result_1
```

Prompt 241
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Define task_1_k_static = 17.
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs k = task_1_k_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], k: int) -> int:
    s = mx_pre = mi_pre = mx_sub = 0
    for x in task_1_input:
        s += x
        mx_pre = max(mx_pre, s)
        mi_pre = min(mi_pre, s)
        mx_sub = max(mx_sub, s - mi_pre)
    ans = mx_sub
    mod = 10 ** 9 + 7
    if k == 1:
        python_result_1 = ans % mod
        return python_result_1
    mx_suf = s - mi_pre
    ans = max(ans, mx_pre + mx_suf)
    if s > 0:
        ans = max(ans, (k - 2) * s + mx_pre + mx_suf)
    python_result_1 = ans % mod
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 249
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Define task_1_capacityA_static = -96.
Define task_1_capacityB_static = 75.
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs capacityA = task_1_capacityA_static, capacityB = task_1_capacityB_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], capacityA: int, capacityB: int) -> int:
    (a, b) = (capacityA, capacityB)
    ans = 0
    (i, j) = (0, len(task_1_input) - 1)
    while i < j:
        if a < task_1_input[i]:
            ans += 1
            a = capacityA
        a -= task_1_input[i]
        if b < task_1_input[j]:
            ans += 1
            b = capacityB
        b -= task_1_input[j]
        (i, j) = (i + 1, j - 1)
    ans += i == j and max(a, b) < task_1_input[i]
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs capacityA = task_1_capacityA_static, capacityB = task_1_capacityB_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], capacityA: int, capacityB: int) -> int:
    (a, b) = (capacityA, capacityB)
    ans = 0
    (i, j) = (0, len(task_1_input) - 1)
    while i < j:
        if a < task_1_input[i]:
            ans += 1
            a = capacityA
        a -= task_1_input[i]
        if b < task_1_input[j]:
            ans += 1
            b = capacityB
        b -= task_1_input[j]
        (i, j) = (i + 1, j - 1)
    ans += i == j and max(a, b) < task_1_input[i]
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    ans = 0
    (i, n) = (0, len(task_1_input))
    while i < n:
        j = i + 1
        while j < n and task_1_input[j - 1] - task_1_input[j] == 1:
            j += 1
        cnt = j - i
        ans += (1 + cnt) * cnt // 2
        i = j
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Define task_1_nums2_static = [-52, 36, -50, -42, -11, -7, 71, 68, -63, 96, -15, 64, -39, 84, -77, -34, 83, -42, 96, -90, -26, -20, -24, 87, 15, 70, 45, -78, 92, 30].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs nums2 = task_1_nums2_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], nums2: List[int]) -> int:
    nums = [a - b for (a, b) in zip(task_1_input, nums2)]
    nums.sort()
    (l, r) = (0, len(nums) - 1)
    ans = 0
    while l < r:
        while l < r and nums[l] + nums[r] <= 0:
            l += 1
        ans += r - l
        r -= 1
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    right = [n] * n
    stk = []
    for i in range(n - 1, -1, -1):
        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] >= task_1_input[i]:
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            right[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(i)
    python_result_1 = sum((j - i for (i, j) in enumerate(right)))
    return python_result_1
```

Prompt 281
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Define task_1_target_static = 19.
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs target = task_1_target_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], target: int) -> int:
    task_1_input.sort()
    (ans, n) = (0, len(task_1_input))
    for i in range(n - 2):
        (j, k) = (i + 1, n - 1)
        while j < k:
            x = task_1_input[i] + task_1_input[j] + task_1_input[k]
            if x < target:
                ans += k - j
                j += 1
            else:
                k -= 1
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

```python
        (j, k) = (i + 1, n - 1)
        while j < k:
```
```python
            x = nums[i] + nums[j] + nums[k]
            if x < task_1_input:
                ans += k - j
                j += 1
            else:
                k -= 1
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 297
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> List[int]:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    left = [-1] * n
    right = [n] * n
    stk = []
    for (i, x) in enumerate(task_1_input):
        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] <= x:
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            left[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(i)
    stk = []
    for i in range(n - 1, -1, -1):
        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] <= task_1_input[i]:
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            right[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(i)
    python_result_1 = [r - l - 1 for (l, r) in zip(left, right)]
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 305
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Define task_1_nums2_static = [21, 23, 81, -51, -14, -89, 55, 18, -86, -81, 73, -61, 21, -18, -31, 35, 62, -22, 70, 65, -4, -85, 31, -54, 66, -39, 77, -57, 6, 50].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs nums2 = task_1_nums2_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], nums2: List[int]) -> int:
    mod = 10 ** 9 + 7
    (m, n) = (len(task_1_input), len(nums2))
    i = j = 0
    f = g = 0
    while i < m or j < n:
        if i == m:
            g += nums2[j]
            j += 1
        elif j == n:
            f += task_1_input[i]
            i += 1
        elif task_1_input[i] < nums2[j]:
            f += task_1_input[i]
            i += 1
        elif task_1_input[i] > nums2[j]:
            g += nums2[j]
            j += 1
        else:
            f = g = max(f, g) + task_1_input[i]
            i += 1
            j += 1
    python_result_1 = max(f, g) % mod
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 329
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> List[int]:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    ans = [0] * n
    stk = []
    for i in range(n - 1, -1, -1):
        while stk and stk[-1] < task_1_input[i]:
            ans[i] += 1
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            ans[i] += 1
        stk.append(task_1_input[i])
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> List[int]:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    leftSum = [0] * n
    for i in range(1, n):
        leftSum[i] = leftSum[i - 1] + task_1_input[i - 1]
    total = sum(task_1_input)
    answer = []
    for i in range(n):
        rightSum_i = total - task_1_input[i] - leftSum[i]
        answer.append(abs(leftSum[i] - rightSum_i))
    python_result_1 = answer
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

======================================================================
Prompt 345
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    (i, n) = (0, len(task_1_input))
    while i + 1 < n and task_1_input[i] < task_1_input[i + 1]:
        i += 1
    if i == n - 1:
        python_result_1 = n * (n + 1) // 2
        return python_result_1
    ans = i + 2
    j = n - 1
    while j:
        while i >= 0 and task_1_input[i] >= task_1_input[j]:
            i -= 1
        ans += i + 2
        if task_1_input[j - 1] >= task_1_input[j]:
            break
        j -= 1
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 353
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
def task_1(task_1_input: int) -> int:
    (s, k) = (0, 1)
    while s + k * (k + 1) // 2 <= task_1_input:
        s += k * (k + 1) // 2
        k += 1
    k -= 1
    ans = k * (k + 1) // 2
    k = 1
    while s < task_1_input:
        ans += 1
        s += k
        k += 1
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Define task_1_trainers_static = [-46, -90, -91, 73, -58, -93, 18, -22, 40, -56, -8, -62, -100, -74, 99, 5, -26, 79, 19, 73, -83, 64, -86, 74, -82, 97, -29, -36, -14, -44].

```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], trainers: List[int]) -> int:
    task_1_input.sort()
    trainers.sort()
    (j, n) = (0, len(trainers))
    for (i, p) in enumerate(task_1_input):
        while j < n and trainers[j] < p:
            j += 1
        if j == n:
            python_result_1 = i
            return python_result_1
        j += 1
    python_result_1 = len(task_1_input)
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.

Prompt 369
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    max_total = 0
    for i in range(n):
        left = [0] * (i + 1)
        left[i] = task_1_input[i]
        for j in range(i - 1, -1, -1):
            left[j] = min(task_1_input[j], left[j + 1])
        left_sum = sum(left)
        right_length = n - i
        right = [0] * right_length
        right[0] = task_1_input[i]
        for j in range(i + 1, n):
            pos_in_right = j - i
            right[pos_in_right] = min(task_1_input[j], right[pos_in_right - 1])
        right_sum = sum(right)
        current_total = left_sum + right_sum - task_1_input[i]
        if current_total > max_total:
            max_total = current_total
    python_result_1 = max_total
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 377
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    stk = []
    left = [-1] * n
    right = [n] * n
    for (i, h) in enumerate(task_1_input):
        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] >= h:
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            left[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(i)
    stk = []
    for i in range(n - 1, -1, -1):
        h = task_1_input[i]
        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] >= h:
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            right[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(i)
    python_result_1 = max((h * (right[i] - left[i] - 1) for (i, h) in enumerate(task_1_input)))
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    stk = []
    left = [-1] * n
    right = [n] * n
    for (i, h) in enumerate(task_1_input):
        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] >= h:
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            left[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(i)
    stk = []
    for i in range(n - 1, -1, -1):
        h = task_1_input[i]
        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] >= h:
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            right[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(i)
    python_result_1 = max((h * (right[i] - left[i] - 1) for (i, h) in enumerate(task_1_input)))
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    stk = []
```
```python
    for (i, x) in enumerate(task_1_input):
        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] <= x:
            stk.pop()
        stk.append(i)
    ans = i = 0
    for j in stk:
        ans += task_1_input[j] * (j - i)
        i = j
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```

Prompt 393
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Define task_1_k_static = 96.
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs k = task_1_k_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], k: int) -> int:
    if task_1_input[0] > k:
        python_result_1 = k
        return python_result_1
    (left, right) = (0, len(task_1_input))
    while left < right:
        mid = left + right >> 1
        if task_1_input[mid] - mid - 1 >= k:
            right = mid
        else:
            left = mid + 1
    python_result_1 = task_1_input[left - 1] + k - (task_1_input[left - 1] - (left - 1) - 1)
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

```python
from typing import List
```

```python
        python_result_1 = task_1_input
        return python_result_1
    (left, right) = (0, len(arr))

            left = mid + 1

    return python_result_1
```

Prompt 409
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    f = [[0] * n for _ in range(n)]
    g = [[0] * n for _ in range(n)]
    for j in range(1, n - 2):
        cnt = sum((task_1_input[l] > task_1_input[j] for l in range(j + 1, n)))
        for k in range(j + 1, n - 1):
            if task_1_input[j] > task_1_input[k]:
                f[j][k] = cnt
            else:
                cnt -= 1
    for k in range(2, n - 1):
        cnt = sum((task_1_input[i] < task_1_input[k] for i in range(k)))
        for j in range(k - 1, 0, -1):
            if task_1_input[j] > task_1_input[k]:
                g[j][k] = cnt
            else:
                cnt -= 1
    python_result_1 = sum((f[j][k] * g[j][k] for j in range(1, n - 2) for k in range(j + 1, n - 1)))
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 417
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    left = [-1] * n
    right = [n] * n
    stk = []
    for (i, v) in enumerate(task_1_input):
        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] >= v:
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            left[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(i)
    stk = []
    for i in range(n - 1, -1, -1):
        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] > task_1_input[i]:
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            right[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(i)
    mod = 10 ** 9 + 7
    python_result_1 = sum(((i - left[i]) * (right[i] - i) * v for (i, v) in enumerate(task_1_input))) % mod
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

```python
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            left[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(i)
    stk = []

        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] > task_1_input[i]:
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            right[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(i)
    mod = 10 ** 9 + 7
    python_result_1 = sum(((i - left[i]) * (right[i] - i) * v for (i, v) in enumerate(task_1_input))) % mod
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    left = [-1] * n
    right = [n] * n
    stk = []
    for (i, v) in enumerate(task_1_input):
        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] >= v:
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            left[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(i)
    stk = []
    for i in range(n - 1, -1, -1):
        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] > task_1_input[i]:
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            right[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(i)
    mod = 10 ** 9 + 7
    python_result_1 = sum(((i - left[i]) * (right[i] - i) * v for (i, v) in enumerate(task_1_input))) % mod
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 425
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    mod = 10 ** 9 + 7
    n = len(task_1_input)
    task_1_input.sort()
    idx = {v: i for (i, v) in enumerate(task_1_input)}
    f = [1] * n
    for (i, a) in enumerate(task_1_input):
        for j in range(i):
            b = task_1_input[j]
            if a % b == 0 and (c := (a // b)) in idx:
                f[i] = (f[i] + f[j] * f[idx[c]]) % mod
    python_result_1 = sum(f) % mod
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
```
```python
    k = 3
    n = len(task_1_input)
    ans = cnt = 0
    for i in range(n << 1):
        if i and task_1_input[i % n] == task_1_input[(i - 1) % n]:
            cnt = 1
        else:
            cnt += 1
        ans += i >= n and cnt >= k
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
```
```python
    stk = []
    for v in task_1_input:
        if not stk or v >= stk[-1]:
            stk.append(v)
        else:
            mx = stk.pop()
            while stk and stk[-1] > v:
                stk.pop()
            stk.append(mx)
    python_result_1 = len(stk)
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.

```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    stk = []
    for v in task_1_input:
        if not stk or v >= stk[-1]:
            stk.append(v)
        else:
            mx = stk.pop()
            while stk and stk[-1] > v:
                stk.pop()
            stk.append(mx)
    python_result_1 = len(stk)
    return python_result_1
```

======================================================================
Prompt 449
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    stk = []
    left = [-1] * n
    for (i, x) in enumerate(task_1_input):
        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] > x:
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            left[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(i)
    stk = []
    right = [n] * n
    for i in range(n - 1, -1, -1):
        x = task_1_input[i]
        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] >= x:
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            right[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(i)
    f = [0] * n
    for (i, x) in enumerate(task_1_input):
        if i and x >= task_1_input[i - 1]:
            f[i] = f[i - 1] + x
        else:
            j = left[i]
            f[i] = x * (i - j) + (f[j] if j != -1 else 0)
    g = [0] * n
    for i in range(n - 1, -1, -1):
        if i < n - 1 and task_1_input[i] >= task_1_input[i + 1]:
            g[i] = g[i + 1] + task_1_input[i]
        else:
            j = right[i]
            g[i] = task_1_input[i] * (j - i) + (g[j] if j != n else 0)
    python_result_1 = max((a + b - c for (a, b, c) in zip(f, g, task_1_input)))
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 457
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    def dfs(nums):
        if len(nums) < 2:
            return 1
        left = [x for x in nums if x < nums[0]]
        right = [x for x in nums if x > nums[0]]
        (m, n) = (len(left), len(right))
        (a, b) = (dfs(left), dfs(right))
        return c[m + n][m] * a % mod * b % mod
    n = len(task_1_input)
    mod = 10 ** 9 + 7
    c = [[0] * n for _ in range(n)]
    c[0][0] = 1
    for i in range(1, n):
        c[i][0] = 1
        for j in range(1, i + 1):
            c[i][j] = (c[i - 1][j] + c[i - 1][j - 1]) % mod
    python_result_1 = (dfs(task_1_input) - 1 + mod) % mod
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    def dfs(nums):
        if len(nums) < 2:
            return 1
        left = [x for x in nums if x < nums[0]]
        right = [x for x in nums if x > nums[0]]
        (m, n) = (len(left), len(right))
        (a, b) = (dfs(left), dfs(right))
        return c[m + n][m] * a % mod * b % mod
    n = len(task_1_input)
    mod = 10 ** 9 + 7
    c = [[0] * n for _ in range(n)]
    c[0][0] = 1
    for i in range(1, n):
        c[i][0] = 1
        for j in range(1, i + 1):
            c[i][j] = (c[i - 1][j] + c[i - 1][j - 1]) % mod
    python_result_1 = (dfs(task_1_input) - 1 + mod) % mod
    return python_result_1
```

```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    def dfs(nums):
        if len(nums) < 2:
            return 1
        left = [x for x in nums if x < nums[0]]
        right = [x for x in nums if x > nums[0]]
        (m, n) = (len(left), len(right))
        (a, b) = (dfs(left), dfs(right))
        return c[m + n][m] * a % mod * b % mod
    n = len(task_1_input)
    mod = 10 ** 9 + 7
    c = [[0] * n for _ in range(n)]
    c[0][0] = 1
    for i in range(1, n):
        c[i][0] = 1
        for j in range(1, i + 1):
            c[i][j] = (c[i - 1][j] + c[i - 1][j - 1]) % mod
    python_result_1 = (dfs(task_1_input) - 1 + mod) % mod
    return python_result_1
```

```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
```
```python
    ans = t = 1
    for (i, x) in enumerate(task_1_input[1:]):
        if task_1_input[i] < x:
            t += 1
            ans = max(ans, t)
        else:
            t = 1
    t = 1
    for (i, x) in enumerate(task_1_input[1:]):
        if task_1_input[i] > x:
            t += 1
            ans = max(ans, t)
        else:
            t = 1
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```

Prompt 473
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    (i, n) = (0, len(task_1_input))
    while i + 1 < n and task_1_input[i] < task_1_input[i + 1]:
        i += 1
    if i == n - 1:
        python_result_1 = n * (n + 1) // 2
        return python_result_1
    ans = i + 2
    j = n - 1
    while j:
        while i >= 0 and task_1_input[i] >= task_1_input[j]:
            i -= 1
        ans += i + 2
        if task_1_input[j - 1] >= task_1_input[j]:
            break
        j -= 1
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 481
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    f = [1] * n
    cnt = [1] * n
    mx = 0
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(i):
            if task_1_input[j] < task_1_input[i]:
                if f[i] < f[j] + 1:
                    f[i] = f[j] + 1
                    cnt[i] = cnt[j]
                elif f[i] == f[j] + 1:
                    cnt[i] += cnt[j]
        if mx < f[i]:
            mx = f[i]
            ans = cnt[i]
        elif mx == f[i]:
            ans += cnt[i]
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

```python
    mx = 0
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(i):
            if task_1_input[j] < task_1_input[i]:
                if f[i] < f[j] + 1:
                    f[i] = f[j] + 1
                    cnt[i] = cnt[j]
                elif f[i] == f[j] + 1:
                    cnt[i] += cnt[j]
        if mx < f[i]:
            mx = f[i]
            ans = cnt[i]
        elif mx == f[i]:
            ans += cnt[i]
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
```
```python
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> List[int]:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    suf = [0] * (n + 1)
    s = set()
    for i in range(n - 1, -1, -1):
        s.add(task_1_input[i])
        suf[i] = len(s)
    s.clear()
    ans = [0] * n
    for (i, x) in enumerate(task_1_input):
        s.add(x)
        ans[i] = len(s) - suf[i + 1]
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
def task_1(task_1_input: int) -> int:
    ans = 0
    (mi, mx) = (-2 ** 31, 2 ** 31 - 1)
    while task_1_input:
        if ans < mi // 10 + 1 or ans > mx // 10:
            python_result_1 = 0
            return python_result_1
        y = task_1_input % 10
        if task_1_input < 0 and y > 0:
            y -= 10
        ans = ans * 10 + y
        task_1_input = (task_1_input - y) // 10
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.

Prompt 505
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Define task_1_warehouse_static = [-94, -10, 27, -31, 63, 82, 20, 65, 96, 39, -68, 6, -86, -73, 84, 79, 52, 83, -96, 56, 59, 41, -5, 31, -57, 6, -72, 77, 91, 43].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs warehouse = task_1_warehouse_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], warehouse: List[int]) -> int:
    n = len(warehouse)
    left = [warehouse[0]] * n
    for i in range(1, n):
        left[i] = min(left[i - 1], warehouse[i])
    task_1_input.sort()
    (i, j) = (0, n - 1)
    while i < len(task_1_input):
```

Prompt 513

Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Define task_1_initialEnergy_static = -51.
Define task_1_initialExperience_static = -17.
Define task_1_experience_static = [-7, 87, -93, -37, -75, -6, -20, -55, 72, -58, 50, -39, 97, 1, 76, -54, 85, 97, -34, 9, 15, 50, -84, -45, 59, 71, 87, -69, 32, -86].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs initialEnergy = task_1_initialEnergy_static, initialExperience = task_1_initialExperience_static, experience = task_1_experience_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(initialEnergy: int, initialExperience: int, task_1_input: List[int], experience: List[int]) -> int:
    sum_energy = sum(task_1_input)
    energy_hours = max(0, sum_energy + 1 - initialEnergy)
    exp_hours = 0
    current_exp = initialExperience
    for e in experience:
        if current_exp <= e:
            delta = e + 1 - current_exp
            exp_hours += delta
            current_exp += delta
        current_exp += e
    python_result_1 = energy_hours + exp_hours
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Define task_1_initialExperience_static = 20.
Define task_1_energy_static = [35, 75, -59, 22, -10, -15, 31, 32, -9, -14, -96, -82, -36, -53, -67, -91, 64, 82, 11, 49, -16, 77, 84, -55, -8, -78, -14, 100, 41, -98].
Define task_1_experience_static = [-99, -55, -35, -94, 61, -12, -18, 37, 28, -99, -30, -28, -84, 99, 70, -53, -48, 100, -20, 88, 81, -47, -66, -4, -16, -61, 48, -32, 49, -97].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs initialExperience = task_1_initialExperience_static, energy = task_1_energy_static, experience = task_1_experience_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: int, initialExperience: int, energy: List[int], experience: List[int]) -> int:
    sum_energy = sum(energy)
    energy_hours = max(0, sum_energy + 1 - task_1_input)
    exp_hours = 0
    current_exp = initialExperience
    for e in experience:
        if current_exp <= e:
            delta = e + 1 - current_exp
            exp_hours += delta
            current_exp += delta
        current_exp += e
    python_result_1 = energy_hours + exp_hours
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 529
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> List[int]:
    stk = []
    n = len(task_1_input)
    ans = [0] * n
    for i in range(n - 1, -1, -1):
        while stk and task_1_input[stk[-1]] <= task_1_input[i]:
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            ans[i] = stk[-1] - i
        stk.append(i)
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 537
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Define task_1_nums2_static = [-60, -30, 62, 20, -84, -68, -89, 74, 21, -11, 35, 80, -4, -66, 93, 64, 100, -95, -43, 57, 29, 39, 96, 35, -15, -89, 49, 72, -39, -25].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs nums2 = task_1_nums2_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], nums2: List[int]) -> int:
    def f(nums1, nums2):
        d = [a - b for (a, b) in zip(nums1, nums2)]
        t = mx = d[0]
        for v in d[1:]:
            if t > 0:
                t += v
            else:
                t = v
            mx = max(mx, t)
        return mx
    (s1, s2) = (sum(task_1_input), sum(nums2))
    python_result_1 = max(s2 + f(task_1_input, nums2), s1 + f(nums2, task_1_input))
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs nums2 = task_1_nums2_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], nums2: List[int]) -> int:
    def f(nums1, nums2):
        d = [a - b for (a, b) in zip(nums1, nums2)]
        t = mx = d[0]
        for v in d[1:]:
            if t > 0:
                t += v
            else:
                t = v
            mx = max(mx, t)
        return mx
    (s1, s2) = (sum(task_1_input), sum(nums2))
    python_result_1 = max(s2 + f(task_1_input, nums2), s1 + f(nums2, task_1_input))
    return python_result_1
```

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    s = set()
    for n in task_1_input:
        i = 2
        while i <= n // i:
            if n % i == 0:
                s.add(i)
                while n % i == 0:
                    n //= i
            i += 1
        if n > 1:
            s.add(n)
    python_result_1 = len(s)
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.

Prompt 553
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    f = [0] * n
    g = [0] * n
    f[0] = int(task_1_input[0] > 0)
    g[0] = int(task_1_input[0] < 0)
    ans = f[0]
    for i in range(1, n):
        if task_1_input[i] > 0:
            f[i] = f[i - 1] + 1
            g[i] = 0 if g[i - 1] == 0 else g[i - 1] + 1
        elif task_1_input[i] < 0:
            f[i] = 0 if g[i - 1] == 0 else g[i - 1] + 1
            g[i] = f[i - 1] + 1
        ans = max(ans, f[i])
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Define task_1_y_static = -3.
Define task_1_bound_static = 31.
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs y = task_1_y_static, bound = task_1_bound_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: int, y: int, bound: int) -> List[int]:
    ans = []
    a = 1
    while a <= bound:
        b = 1
        while a + b <= bound:
            ans.append(a + b)
            b *= y
            if y == 1:
                break
        if task_1_input == 1:
            break
        a *= task_1_input
    python_result_1 = list(ans)
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

```python
        b = 1
        while a + b <= bound:
            ans.append(a + b)
            b *= y
            if y == 1:
                break
        if task_1_input == 1:
            break
        a *= task_1_input
    python_result_1 = list(ans)
    return python_result_1
```

Prompt 569
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> int:
    nums = [v - i for (i, v) in enumerate(task_1_input)]
    n = len(nums)
    left = [-1] * n
    stk = []
    for (i, v) in enumerate(nums):
        while stk and nums[stk[-1]] >= v:
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            left[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(i)
    ans = 0
    dp = [0] * n
    dp[0] = task_1_input[0]
    for (i, v) in enumerate(task_1_input):
        j = left[i]
        cnt = min(v, i - j)
        u = v - cnt + 1
        s = (u + v) * cnt // 2
        dp[i] = s + (0 if j == -1 else dp[j])
        ans = max(ans, dp[i])
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

```python
from typing import List
```
```python
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> List[int]:
    n = len(task_1_input)
    ans = [-1] * n
    stk = []
    for i in range(n * 2 - 1, -1, -1):
        i %= n
        while stk and stk[-1] <= task_1_input[i]:
            stk.pop()
        if stk:
            ans[i] = stk[-1]
        stk.append(task_1_input[i])
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```

Prompt 585
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Define task_1_power_static = 14.
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs power = task_1_power_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], power: int) -> int:
    task_1_input.sort()
    ans = score = 0
    (i, j) = (0, len(task_1_input) - 1)
    while i <= j:
        if power >= task_1_input[i]:
            power -= task_1_input[i]
            (score, i) = (score + 1, i + 1)
            ans = max(ans, score)
        elif score:
            power += task_1_input[j]
            (score, j) = (score - 1, j - 1)
        else:
            break
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Define task_1_power_static = -97.
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs power = task_1_power_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], power: int) -> int:
    task_1_input.sort()
    ans = score = 0
    (i, j) = (0, len(task_1_input) - 1)
    while i <= j:
        if power >= task_1_input[i]:
            power -= task_1_input[i]
            (score, i) = (score + 1, i + 1)
            ans = max(ans, score)
        elif score:
            power += task_1_input[j]
            (score, j) = (score - 1, j - 1)
        else:
            break
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
