Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Define task_1_arr2_static = [-36, 83, -96, -63, 47, 27, 93, -70, -67, 80, -72, 74, 27, -68, 31, -76, 90, -15, -39, 61, -34, 95, -12, 51, 48, -85, -74, 23, 56, -81].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs arr2 = task_1_arr2_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], arr2: List[int]) -> List[int]:
    (i, j) = (len(task_1_input) - 1, len(arr2) - 1)
    c = 0
    ans = []
    while i >= 0 or j >= 0 or c:
        a = 0 if i < 0 else task_1_input[i]
        b = 0 if j < 0 else arr2[j]
        x = a + b + c
        c = 0
        if x >= 2:
            x -= 2
            c -= 1
        elif x == -1:
            x = 1
            c += 1
        ans.append(x)
        (i, j) = (i - 1, j - 1)
    while len(ans) > 1 and ans[-1] == 0:
        ans.pop()
    python_result_1 = ans[::-1]
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Prompt 601
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Define task_1_k_static = -73.
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs k = task_1_k_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], k: int) -> int:
    task_1_input.sort()
    n = len(task_1_input)
    m = n >> 1
    ans = abs(task_1_input[m] - k)
    if task_1_input[m] > k:
        for i in range(m - 1, -1, -1):
            if task_1_input[i] <= k:
                break
            ans += task_1_input[i] - k
    else:
        for i in range(m + 1, n):
            if task_1_input[i] >= k:
                break
            ans += k - task_1_input[i]
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
def task_1(task_1_input: int) -> int:
    while 1:
        (t, s, i) = (task_1_input, 0, 2)
        while i <= task_1_input // i:
            while task_1_input % i == 0:
                task_1_input //= i
                s += i
            i += 1
        if task_1_input > 1:
            s += task_1_input
        if s == t:
            python_result_1 = t
            return python_result_1
        task_1_input = s
```

Prompt 625
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Define task_1_boardingCost_static = -68.
Define task_1_runningCost_static = -42.
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input with inputs boardingCost = task_1_boardingCost_static, runningCost = task_1_runningCost_static.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int], boardingCost: int, runningCost: int) -> int:
    ans = -1
    mx = t = 0
    wait = 0
    i = 0
    while wait or i < len(task_1_input):
        wait += task_1_input[i] if i < len(task_1_input) else 0
        up = wait if wait < 4 else 4
        wait -= up
        t += up * boardingCost - runningCost
        i += 1
        if t > mx:
            mx = t
            ans = i
    python_result_1 = ans
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.

======================================================================
Prompt 633
======================================================================
Solve task 1. Once you have its result, call the submit_answers tool once with the result of task 1.

Task 1:
To get task_1_input, call the get_input tool with task_index=1 and input_dependencies=[].
Trace the execution of task_1 on task_1_input.
```python
from typing import List
def task_1(task_1_input: List[int]) -> List[int]:
    ans = []
    (i, j) = (0, len(task_1_input) - 1)
    while i <= j:
        a = task_1_input[i] * task_1_input[i]
        b = task_1_input[j] * task_1_input[j]
        if a > b:
            ans.append(a)
            i += 1
        else:
            ans.append(b)
            j -= 1
    python_result_1 = ans[::-1]
    return python_result_1
```
Let python_result_1 be the value returned by task_1.
Output the result as task_1_out = {"result": python_result_1}, replacing python_result_1 with its calculated value.
